
## 2026-11-09 — SNC-1 and SNC-2 Stage 0 (both survive their falsification lines)

### What was done (commands, files, features)

- Two Stage 0 checks from `ideas/SYNTHESIS-NC-20261108.md`, run by a Sonnet agent; results in `research/curve25519_autolab_20261104/snc/` [pcc:unverified].
- SNC-2, zero group operations, `src/bin/nc2_stage0.rs --chained`, n = 2000 per curve: chained C = 2 E is 1.734 / 1.722 / 1.718 sqrt(r) at k = 20 / 24 / 28 (SE about 0.0065), against the D0 walk 1.730 / 1.712 / 1.730; i.i.d. control 1.6666 / 1.6613 / 1.6638 against 1.662 ± 0.01. The pre-registered design was committed before any chained run. Below the 1.82 kill line; chaining neither helps nor hurts against D0.
- SNC-1, DERIVED (the wwalk oracle exports no verify counter): verify share of the C = 2 incumbent is 0.067 / 0.023 / 0.005 sqrt(r) at k = 24 / 28 / 32 (3.2% / 1.3% / 0.3% of S), from a regression on `c1/sessions/confirm-k*` inc-c2 records. Above the 0.04 kill line at k = 24 only. `wsum` free rows recompute at most one addition per sum; the box recorded 0 collisions in all 120 k = 20 records, so lazy certification removes nothing there.

### Decisions made (and why)

- Neither record can reach 0.75; both are possible small measured gains on the C = 2 baseline. No ecbench panel was run: the SNC-1 gain is at most about 3% at k = 24, and SNC-2 chained E equals the existing D0 walk, so its gain over the incumbent rests on the panel the records describe.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

- The shared SNC-1/SNC-2 panel (2 x 2 factorial plus rho-neg, 8 targets x 64 rounds) is not run; it needs about 200 lines of Rust (two-seed TargetWalk, wwalk option) and a task card.
- 0.75x is unmet; best index-calculus ratio stays 1.40.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- The agent read the synthesis note from `origin/claude/bsgs-k20` because it was not yet on main; #3738 has since merged.

## 2026-11-09 — Prefix-cached inherited-F4 root (target-free chain links): exact, null


- The user asked to implement caching and dynamic programming. Both already exist (F4Remake root replay; inherited F4). The one untried form was reuse across targets of the *pivot* rows of the chain's target-free links.
- Added `src/cryptanalysis/inherited_f4/prefix.rs` (`ReducedBasis::from_system_with_prefix`). It caches the prefix RREF and Four Russians tables, and per target reduces only the last link. The hook is `KIC_F4_PREFIX_ROOT=1`, off by default. The prefix hint is set in `groebner_decompose_with_geometry`.
- Added the probe `examples/f4_prefix_probe.rs` and a unit test `prefix_root_spans_the_from_scratch_root`.
- Five cells (K0/2^13, K1/2^15 at m = 4; K0/2^15, K1/2^17, K0/2^19 at m = 3): scratch ÷ prefix is 0.989–1.006× in stage word XORs. Verdicts, splits and reductions are identical; row spaces are equal. The study is in `research/f4_prefix_root_20261109/` [pcc:unverified].


- Recorded as a null; the flag stays off. The target-free links are cubic, so at D = 3 they take no multipliers and make up only 4–10 % of the root rows. The root is 3–28 % of the stage.
- No benchmarks/ or leaderboard row: there is no improvement. [pcc:unverified]


- A D ≥ 4 operating point would raise the prefix share. It is untested and would cost more per node.
- Conductor was not installed in this container (`conductor: command not found`), so no task was reserved.


- `f4_lane_probe`/probe defaults (K0/2^17) have no usable subgroup, so cells were chosen from those that construct. Shape output is Rust Debug; `summarize.py` parses it.

## 2026-11-09 — Coefficient size / curve model in prime-field Semaev decomposition (preregistered, running)


- User goal: does S3/S4 relation finding over F_p depend on the curve coefficients (minimal-|a|,|b| isogenous curves, Montgomery/Edwards models) on a reduced ECCp-131 analogue?
- Exact fact E1 (verified from `src/ecc/curve.rs`: ECCp-131 h = 1): every curve isogenous to ECCp-131 has prime order, so no Montgomery or (twisted) Edwards model exists in the class. The model arm is studied only on a separate 4 | #E curve (side study M). [pcc:unverified]
- Study dir `research/coeff_model_semaev_20261109/`: PROTOCOL.md, Rust instrument `tool/` (Montgomery-form F_p, Weierstrass and Montgomery S3 root-finders, S4 by S3 chaining, group-law cross-check, n·P = O class-member filter), `tool/setup.py` (PARI 2.17 via cypari2 confirms every member with ellcard), frozen `tool/analyze.py`. Preregistration committed as e23b0bf before any data (FROZEN.sha256).
- Smoke (24 bits): S3 root-finder count equals group-law count on 300/300 Weierstrass targets and 210/210 side-study targets (Montgomery S3 included).
- C40 curves: p = 1332528136487 (2^40.3), E0 prime order, conductor 1, h = 489272. Smallest-coefficient class members: (209, -977), (1056, 574), (1369, 661), (-597, -1919); a = -3 members b = -2288188, 5086643. Filter cost per random member 7.56M tests (6 found) against 2p/h = 5.45M.
- Running: C40-S3 (19 arms x 1000 targets); C40-S4, C40-SIDE, C48-S3 queued.


- Local L0 execution instead of isolab-eks: no `aws` CLI or EKS client in this container. The deciding metrics (relation counts, Montgomery multiplications) are exact and host-independent; clocks are descriptive only (same footing as `isogeny_class_ic_yield_20261102`).

## 2026-11-09 (final) — Coefficient-model study complete


- The C48-S3 T = 800 run finished (7200/7200 jobs, 8478 s, frozen binary 62380f44…). Replay checks: the 1800 T = 200 rows reproduce exactly (`runs/C48-S3-T800.replay.json`: 0 differences), and the 6164 rows of the killed attempt reproduce exactly.
- `cmsem analyze` on the T = 800 data:
  - P1 = 0.996 [0.910, 1.091].
  - Every family contrast has p_holm = 1.0. SMALL1/RAND = 0.998 (the T = 200 dip of 0.76 regressed away).
  - Heterogeneity p = 0.99.
  - Counted cost is 87.0693 multiplications per element on every arm.
  - The positive control was not detected: 1.116 [0.972, 1.279].
  - Decision under the frozen rule: inconclusive.
- `report/REPORT.md` and `REPORT.pdf` (pandoc → typst) and the figures (`yield_per_arm`, `counted_cost_per_arm`, `reach_cost`) are final.


- Answer to the goal: the class is flat for prime-field S3 decomposition. The C40-S3 primary cell meets the frozen flat rule; C48 shows no effect but is inconclusive by rule.
  - Coefficients enter the S3 kernel through one b·s product, so the possible gain is at most 1 multiplication of about 75.
  - The Montgomery model saves exactly 1 multiplication per element (74.06 vs 75.06) and has no effect on yield.
  - No Montgomery or Edwards model exists in the ECCp-131 class (h = 1).
  - Reaching a small member takes about 2^67.6 candidates. That is above rho only at ≥ 0.15 GAE per test, so the §5a band does not hold across all prices. The conclusion does not rely on it, because the curve gains nothing even when free.
- No further C48 extension (optional stopping). No leaderboard or benchmarks row (characterization study). Claims are labelled [pcc:unverified].


- A larger planted fraction would make a C48-scale positive control powerful. It would need a new preregistration.


- None beyond the earlier time-limit kill (handled by a detached rerun of the frozen binary).

## 2026-11-09T00:00:00Z — Resumable class-group runs (Claude)


- Container restart at 00:24Z killed the first P-192 run (one of four determinant subsets done). Added checkpoints to `class_group_subexp`: the relation pool after every sieving round and each finished subset determinant (`--checkpoint FILE`, files keyed by discriminant, bound, factor-base size and a matrix checksum); `compute` resumes from them; the driver is idempotent (skips finished labels, resumes interrupted ones). Test: a resumed run reproduces the class number without sieving; a checkpoint of another discriminant is ignored.
- Relaunched at 00:37Z detached; the pool checkpoint (6661 relations, 925 KB) was written at 00:40Z; the container was reclaimed at about 00:43Z before any determinant finished. The scheduled check-in of 00:51Z was delivered on 2026-11-10 at 03:38Z: the session had been suspended for the whole day.


- Detached (`setsid nohup`) processes do not survive this runtime's reclaim; runs go through the harness background runner, which has kept the container alive for its two-hour cap, with the checkpoints covering the rest.


- All five P-192 results.


- Two restarts lost about 25 minutes of computation in total; both are now recoverable in ten-minute units.
